<div class='question question'>

Divide: $92 \div 4$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
      & = \dfrac {92}{4}                                  \\\\
      & = \dfrac {4 \times 23}{4}                         \\\\
      & = \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 23}{\cancel 4}         \\\\
      & = 23                                               
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$23$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
      & = \dfrac {92}{4}                                  \\\\
      & = \dfrac {4 \times 23}{4}                         \\\\
      & = \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 23}{\cancel 4}         \\\\
      & = 23                                               
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-531ECA32-EC61-4DC0-B7E2-A82CE25C7F7E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I have $\pounds 8.17$ credit on my mobile phone. At the end of a phone call this falls 
to $\pounds 7.65$. 
How much did the call cost?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of the call}     &= 8.17 - 7.65 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.52
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 0.52$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of the call}     &= 8.17 - 7.65 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.52
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 0.52$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-AFC01A5F-DFE5-4512-A316-799D2FC52ABB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out the product of $528$ and $99$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
    &            &     5        &   2   &   8 \\
    &   \times   &              &   9   &   9 \\
\hline
    &   4        &    7         &   5   &   2 \\
4   &   7        &    5         &   2   &   0 \\
\hline
5   &   2        &    2         &   7   &   2
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$52272$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
    &            &     5        &   2   &   8 \\
    &   \times   &              &   9   &   9 \\
\hline
    &   4        &    7         &   5   &   2 \\
4   &   7        &    5         &   2   &   0 \\
\hline
5   &   2        &    2         &   7   &   2
\end{array}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A01A640F-EDFD-4999-9FB4-D5CB13DE99BE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Barbie earns $\pounds 23450$ a year and her partner Ken earns $\pounds 700$ less than Barbie. 
How much do the couple earn altogether?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total earning} &= 23450 + 23450 - 700 \\\\
                      &= 23450 + 22750 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 46200
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 46200$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total earning} &= 23450 + 23450 - 700 \\\\
                      &= 23450 + 22750 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 46200
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 46200$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4AED5958-F04F-45F1-A4E4-5E09C607BC13'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Convert $7.4 \ \text{kilograms}$ into grams.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{kilograms}   &= 1000 \ \text{grams} \\\\
7.4 \ \text{kilograms} &= 1000 \times 7.4 \\\\
                       &= 7400 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7400 \ \text{grams}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{kilograms}   &= 1000 \ \text{grams} \\\\
7.4 \ \text{kilograms} &= 1000 \times 7.4 \\\\
                       &= 7400 \ \text{grams}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7400 \ \text{grams}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-42B09AC3-796B-4A16-B123-5A18D316FA38'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The opening hours of my local supermarket are $7 \ \text{am}$ to $9 \ \text{pm}$ Mondays to Saturdays and 
$10{:}30 \ \text{am}$ to $4{:}30 \ \text{pm}$ on Sundays. For how many hours does this supermarket open each week?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}    &= 6 \times ( 9{:}00 \ \text{pm} - 7{:}00 \ \text{am} ) + 04{:}30 \ \text{pm} - 10{:}30 \ \text{am} \\\\
                      &= 6 \times 14 \ \text{hours} +  6 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                      &= 84 + 6 \\\\
                      &= 90 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$90 \ \text {hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}    &= 6 \times ( 9{:}00 \ \text{pm} - 7{:}00 \ \text{am} ) + 04{:}30 \ \text{pm} - 10{:}30 \ \text{am} \\\\
                      &= 6 \times 14 \ \text{hours} +  6 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                      &= 84 + 6 \\\\
                      &= 90 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$90 \ \text {hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-60B9091C-D342-4FED-A634-E494AC62D7E8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Arrange the numbers, $5, 3, 7$ and $2$ to make the largest possible four-digit number which is a multiple of $5$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

A multiple of $5$ will have in unit place $5$

Let's arrange rest of the numbers $3, 7$ and $2$ in descending order,

$7 > 3 > 2$

$7$ at thousands-place

$3$ at hundreds-place and 

$2$ at tens-place 

The largest possible four-digit which is a multiple of $5$, using numbers $5, 3, 7$ and $2 = 7325$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7325$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>A multiple of $5$ will have in unit place $5$

Let's arrange rest of the numbers $3, 7$ and $2$ in descending order,

$7 > 3 > 2$

$7$ at thousands-place

$3$ at hundreds-place and 

$2$ at tens-place 

The largest possible four-digit which is a multiple of $5$, using numbers $5, 3, 7$ and $2 = 7325$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7325$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CE97FB1F-AA68-41D1-BD54-0147E9CD7F70'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $0.08$ multiplied by $5$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Let's first multiply the numbers ignoring the decimal points.


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times 5 \\\\
&= 40
\end{aligned}
$

Let's account for the decimal point now.

$= 0.4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_multiply_when_decimal_points_note

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times 5 \\\\
&= 40
\end{aligned}
$

Let's account for the decimal point now.

$= 0.4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.4$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C74F6145-1D9D-471D-9081-11DC74346EC6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The new TV channel, Lazy Living, broadcasts for $98 \ \text{hours}$ a week. 
If two-sevenths of its output is devoted to make-over programmes and the rest to celebrity gossip, 
for how many hours each week does the channel broadcast programmes on celebrity gossip?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


Let's find the total hours of celebrity gossip's broadcasted by the channel each week.

$
\begin{aligned}
      &= 98 - \dfrac {2} {7} \times 98 \ \text{hours} \\\\
      &= 98 \times (1 - \dfrac {2} {7}) \\\\
      &= 98 \times (\dfrac{1 \times 7}{1 \times 7} - \dfrac {2}{7}) \\\\
      &= 98 \times (\dfrac {5} {7}) \\\\
      & = \dfrac {5 \times 14 \times 7 } {7}  \\\\
      & = \dfrac {5 \times 14 \times \cancel 7 } {\cancel 7} \\\\
      & = 5 \times 14  \\\\
      & = 70 \ \text{hours}                                
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$70 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
Let's find the total hours of celebrity gossip's broadcasted by the channel each week.

$
\begin{aligned}
      &= 98 - \dfrac {2} {7} \times 98 \ \text{hours} \\\\
      &= 98 \times (1 - \dfrac {2} {7}) \\\\
      &= 98 \times (\dfrac{1 \times 7}{1 \times 7} - \dfrac {2}{7}) \\\\
      &= 98 \times (\dfrac {5} {7}) \\\\
      & = \dfrac {5 \times 14 \times 7 } {7}  \\\\
      & = \dfrac {5 \times 14 \times \cancel 7 } {\cancel 7} \\\\
      & = 5 \times 14  \\\\
      & = 70 \ \text{hours}                                
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$70 \ \text{hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Jonathan thinks that the shortest time taken for me to prepare my breakfast is $6 \ \text{minutes}$. Explain briefly why Jonathan is wrong.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task. 

The coffee making takes $2 \ \text{minutes}$, which I can do while my bread is getting toasted in the toaster, which takes $3 \ \text{minutes}$. 

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\underline {{\text {Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task.}}}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Jonathan is wrong because I can manage making a coffee in a machine and toasting the breast in a toaster as a parallel task. 

The coffee making takes $2 \ \text{minutes}$, which I can do while my bread is getting toasted in the toaster, which takes $3 \ \text{minutes}$. 
</code></pre>

</div>
</div>

</li>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the correct shortest time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The shortest time taken by me to prepare my breakfast is,

$
\begin{aligned}
      & = \text {Brown the toast in toaster + Butter the toast} \\\\
      & = 3 + 1                                                 \\\\
      & = 4 \ \text{minutes}                               
\end{aligned}
$

The shortest time taken by me to prepare my breakfast is $=4 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-36CD4F24-0C6D-4BE2-ADC6-E8633C0E7C8A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The chart below shows the mileage between five places in North London. For example it gives the distance between Kenton and 
Hampstead as $4 \ \text{miles}$.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


Daisy lives in Hampstead and drives her son to school at Haberdashers' in the morning before continuing on to the office in Southgate. 
She then visits her sister in Harpenden before driving home via Habs. How far does she travel altogether?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

For such questions, it will be easier to write down the route taken.


$
\begin{matrix}
\text {Hampstead}  &\rightarrow&     \text {Habs},  &\rightarrow&     \text{ Southgate},   &\rightarrow&     \text{ Harpenden}  &\rightarrow&   \text {via Habs}  &\rightarrow&     \text {Hampsted} \\
(home)  &&      (school)  &&      (office)  &&      (sister)  &&     &&     (home) \\\\
&   \footnotesize{\maroonC{(10)}} 
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+9)}}  
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+18)}}   
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+13)}}  
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+10)}} \\
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
      & = 10 + 9 + 18 + 13 + 10         \\\\
      & = 60                             \\\\
      & = 60 \ \text{miles}                               
\end{aligned}
$

She travelled altogether $= 60 \ \text{miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

 $= 60 \ \text{miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>For such questions, it will be easier to write down the route taken.


$
\begin{matrix}
\text {Hampstead}  &\rightarrow&     \text {Habs},  &\rightarrow&     \text{ Southgate},   &\rightarrow&     \text{ Harpenden}  &\rightarrow&   \text {via Habs}  &\rightarrow&     \text {Hampsted} \\
(home)  &&      (school)  &&      (office)  &&      (sister)  &&     &&     (home) \\\\
&   \footnotesize{\maroonC{(10)}} 
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+9)}}  
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+18)}}   
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+13)}}  
&&  \footnotesize{\maroonC{(+10)}} \\
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
      & = 10 + 9 + 18 + 13 + 10         \\\\
      & = 60                             \\\\
      & = 60 \ \text{miles}                               
\end{aligned}
$

She travelled altogether $= 60 \ \text{miles}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'> $= 60 \ \text{miles}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-12F567E6-42DC-4EE3-A2CE-D09D970305D7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The rate of VAT (value-added tax) in this country is $20\%$. In the shop CoCost, the price of a TV excluding VAT is 
$\pounds 550$. Work out the cost of the TV after the VAT is added on.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 550 + 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20} {100} \times 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20 \times 550} {100} \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 55 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 550 + 2 \times 55 \\\\
                    &= 550 + 110 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 660
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 660$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 550 + 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20} {100} \times 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20 \times 550} {100} \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 55 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 550 + 2 \times 55 \\\\
                    &= 550 + 110 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 660
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 660$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The price of a diamond ring, including VAT is $\pounds 1200$. Work out the cost of the ring before the VAT was added on. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost of the ring excluding VAT}  &= 1200 - 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 1200 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20 \times 1200} {100} \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20 \times 12 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - 20 \times 12 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - 240 \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 960
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 960$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost of the ring excluding VAT}  &= 1200 - 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 1200 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20 \times 1200} {100} \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20 \times 12 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - 20 \times 12 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - 240 \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 960
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 960$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-922BF11C-6CA4-42A0-9968-DD93678A9BF4'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Amar (form captain), Brian (vice captain), Charles and Daniel are best friends in the same class at school. 
They always like to stand next to each other in the lunch queue. 

On Mondays it is a school rule that the form captain is at the front of the queue followed by the vice captain. 
There are two ways in which these boys can queue up on a Monday: $ABCD$ and $ABDC$. 

On Tuesdays, the form captain must again queue up first but the remaining three boys can follow in any order. 
There are six ways in which these boys can queue up on a Tuesday. Four of these ways are listed below. 
Write down the remaining two: $ABCD, ABDC, ACBD, ACDB, \text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore}, \text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore}$ 

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0DB24821-F222-4AA3-A90B-8A08F41EC2D7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

As part of a mathematics project a class is asked to look up the lengths of objects on the internet.

These objects are listed in the rectangles below and the measurements are given in the triangles.

Unfortunately these objects and measurements have been muddled up.

Draw lines on the diagram to match each object with its correct length.

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


Width of Wembley Stadium pitch
$300 \ \text{mm}$
Height of an average man
$5600 \ \text{km}$
Distance from London to New York
$135 \ \text{m}$
Length of a piece of standard $A4$ paper
$1750 \ \text{mm}$
Height of the London Eye
$8800 \ \text{m}$
Height of Mount Everest
$6900 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

|                     Object                   |     Correct Length     |
|:--------------------------------------------:|:----------------------:|
|        Width of Wembley Stadium pitch        |   $6900 \ \text{cm}$   |

|       Distance from London to New York       |   $5600 \ \text{km}$   |
|   Length of a piece of standard $A4$ paper   |   $300 \ \text{mm}$    |
|           Height of the London Eye           |    $135 \ \text{m}$    |
|           Height of Mount Everest            |   $8800 \ \text{m}$    |

</div>
</div>
